            node.generators[0].iter = self.visit(node.generators[0].iter)
        self._push_comprehension_scope(node)
        if isinstance(node, ast.DictComp):
            node.key = self.visit(node.key)
            node.value = self.visit(node.value)
        else:
            node.elt = self.visit(node.elt)
        for index, gen in enumerate(node.generators):
            gen.target = self.visit(gen.target)
            gen.ifs = [self.visit(cond) for cond in gen.ifs]
            if index > 0:
                gen.iter = self.visit(gen.iter)
        self.scopes.pop()
        return node

    def visit_ListComp(self, node):
        return self._visit_comprehension(node)

    def visit_SetComp(self, node):
        return self._visit_comprehension(node)

    def visit_DictComp(self, node):
        return self._visit_comprehension(node)

    def visit_GeneratorExp(self, node):
        return self._visit_comprehension(node)

    def visit_Nonlocal(self, node):
        # A nonlocal declaration names the variable in an enclosing
        # function, which has been renamed: rewrite it to match, or
        # the declaration would bind nothing and fail to compile.
        node.names = [self._resolve(name) or name for name in node.names]
        return node

    def visit_Name(self, node):
        new_id = self._resolve(node.id)
        if new_id is not None:
            if new_id != node.id:
                self.renamed += 1
            node.id = new_id
        return node

    def visit_arg(self, node):
        new_id = self._resolve(node.arg)
        if new_id is not None:
            node.arg = new_id
        return node

    def visit_ExceptHandler(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        if node.name:
            new_id = self._resolve(node.name)
            if new_id is not None:
                node.name = new_id
        return node


def _all_args(node):
    """Parameter names of a function definition, in declaration order."""
    args = node.args
    names = [a.arg for a in args.posonlyargs]
    names += [a.arg for a in args.args]
    if args.vararg:
        names.append(args.vararg.arg)
    names += [a.arg for a in args.kwonlyargs]
    if args.kwarg:
        names.append(args.kwarg.arg)
    return names


def _target_names(target):
    """Names bound by an assignment target (handles tuple unpacking)."""
    if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
        return [target.id]
    if isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List)):
        names = []
        for elt in target.elts:
            names.extend(_target_names(elt))
        return names
    return []


def obfuscate_source(source):
    """Obfuscate *source*; return ``(new_source, stats)``."""
    obfuscator = Obfuscator()
    tree = obfuscator.visit(ast.parse(source))
    ast.fix_missing_locations(tree)
    stats = {"renamed": obfuscator.renamed,
             "docstrings_removed": obfuscator.docstrings_removed,
             "keywords_rewritten": obfuscator.keywords_rewritten}
    return ast.unparse(tree), stats


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Demonstration programs.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DEMO_PROGRAMS = {
    "statistics": '''
"""A tiny statistics helper module."""

def mean(values):
    """Return the arithmetic mean of values."""
    total = 0
    count = 0
    for value in values:
        total += value
        count += 1
    return total / count if count else 0

def variance(values):
    """Return the population variance of values."""
    average = mean(values)
    squared = [(value - average) ** 2 for value in values]
    return mean(squared)

print(mean([1, 2, 3, 4, 5]))
print(round(variance([1, 2, 3, 4, 5]), 2))
print(mean([]))
''',
    "text_processor": '''
def word_frequencies(text):
    """Count how often each word appears."""
    frequencies = {}
    for word in text.lower().split():
        cleaned = word.strip(".,!?")
        frequencies[cleaned] = frequencies.get(cleaned, 0) + 1
    return frequencies

def most_common(frequencies, limit=3):
    ranked = sorted(frequencies.items(), key=lambda pair: pair[1], reverse=True)
    return ranked[:limit]

sample = "the cat sat on the mat and the cat slept"
print(word_frequencies(sample))
print(most_common(word_frequencies(sample)))
print(most_common(word_frequencies(sample), 2))
''',
    "closures_and_classes": '''
def make_adder(base):
    """Build a function that adds base to its argument."""
    def adder(amount):
        return base + amount
    return adder

class Rectangle:
    """A rectangle with width and height."""

    def __init__(self, width, height):
        self.width = width
        self.height = height

    def area(self):
        return self.width * self.height

    def scale(self, factor):
        scaled_width = self.width * factor
        scaled_height = self.height * factor
        return Rectangle(scaled_width, scaled_height)

add_five = make_adder(5)
print(add_five(10))
box = Rectangle(3, 4)
print(box.area())
print(box.scale(2).area())
''',
    "loops_and_exceptions": '''
def safe_divide(pairs):
    results = []
    for numerator, denominator in pairs:
        try:
            results.append(numerator / denominator)
        except ZeroDivisionError as problem:
            print("skipped division by zero:", problem)
            results.append(None)
    return results

def find_first(items, wanted):
    for index, item in enumerate(items):
        if item == wanted:
            return index
    return -1

print(safe_divide([(10, 2), (7, 0), (9, 3)]))
print(find_first(["a", "b", "c"], "b"))
print(find_first(["a", "b", "c"], "z"))
''',
}


def demo():
    """Run every demo program through the obfuscator and report."""
    print("=" * 70)
    print("Obfuscator / minifier demo")
    print("=" * 70)
    for name, source in DEMO_PROGRAMS.items():
        obfuscated, stats = obfuscate_source(source)
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator, label=name)
        print("\n--- %s: renamed %d name occurrence(s), "
              "docstrings removed %d ---"
              % (name, stats["renamed"], stats["docstrings_removed"]))
        print("original chars: %d -> obfuscated chars: %d"
              % (len(source), len(obfuscated)))
        print(obfuscated.rstrip())
        assert len(obfuscated) < len(source), "obfuscation did not shrink " + name
        assert '"""' not in obfuscated, "docstring survived in " + name
    print("\nAll behaviour-preservation checks passed; every program "
          "got shorter and lost its docstrings.")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Unit tests.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestObfuscator(unittest.TestCase):
    def _obf(self, source):
        return obfuscate_source(source)[0]

    def test_locals_renamed(self):
        folded = self._obf(
            "def f(first, second):\n"
            "    total = first + second\n"
            "    return total\n")
        self.assertNotIn("first", folded)
        self.assertNotIn("second", folded)
        self.assertNotIn("total", folded)
        self.assertIn("def f(a, b):", folded)

    def test_module_level_names_kept(self):
        folded = self._obf("def helper(x):\n    return x + 1\nprint(helper(41))\n")
        self.assertIn("def helper(", folded)
        self.assertIn("print(helper(41))", folded)

    def test_docstring_removed(self):
        folded = self._obf('def f():\n    """Doc."""\n    return 1\n')
        self.assertNotIn('"""', folded)
        self.assertNotIn("Doc.", folded)

    def test_closure_shares_outer_name(self):
        source = (
            "def outer(base):\n"
            "    def inner(amount):\n"
            "        return base + amount\n"
            "    return inner(10)\n"
            "print(outer(5))\n"
        )
        _, _, new_out = check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)
        self.assertIn("15", new_out)

    def test_shadowing(self):
        source = (
            "def f(value):\n"
            "    def g(value):\n"
            "        return value * 2\n"
            "    return g(value) + value\n"
            "print(f(10))\n"
        )
        _, _, new_out = check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)
        self.assertIn("30", new_out)

    def test_global_not_renamed(self):
        source = (
            "counter = 0\n"
            "def bump():\n"
            "    global counter\n"
            "    counter += 1\n"
            "    return counter\n"
            "print(bump())\n"
            "print(bump())\n"
        )
        _, _, new_out = check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)
        self.assertIn("1\n2", new_out)

    def test_nonlocal_not_renamed(self):
        source = (
            "def outer():\n"
            "    count = 0\n"
            "    def inner():\n"
            "        nonlocal count\n"
            "        count += 1\n"
            "        return count\n"
            "    return inner() + inner()\n"
            "print(outer())\n"
        )
        _, _, new_out = check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)
        self.assertIn("3", new_out)

    def test_attributes_and_builtins_kept(self):
        source = (
            "class C:\n"
            "    def __init__(self):\n"
            "        self.items = []\n"
            "    def add(self, thing):\n"
            "        self.items.append(thing)\n"
            "        return len(self.items)\n"
            "print(C().add('x'))\n"
        )
        folded = self._obf(source)
        self.assertIn(".items", folded)
        self.assertIn(".append(", folded)
        self.assertIn("len(", folded)
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_comprehension_scope(self):
        source = (
            "def f(numbers):\n"
            "    doubled = [n * 2 for n in numbers]\n"
            "    return doubled\n"
            "print(f([1, 2, 3]))\n"
        )
        folded = self._obf(source)
        self.assertNotIn("doubled", folded)
        self.assertNotIn("numbers", folded)
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_loop_and_exception_names(self):
        source = (
            "def f(pairs):\n"
            "    out = []\n"
            "    for key, val in pairs:\n"
            "        try:\n"
            "            out.append(val // key)\n"
            "        except ZeroDivisionError as err:\n"
            "            out.append(str(err))\n"
            "    return out\n"
            "print(f([(2, 10), (0, 5)]))\n"
        )
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_keyword_arguments_rewritten(self):
        source = (
            "def greet(name, punctuation='!'):\n"
            "    return 'Hello, ' + name + punctuation\n"
            "print(greet(name='Ada'))\n"
            "print(greet('Grace', punctuation='?'))\n"
        )
        folded = self._obf(source)
        self.assertIn("greet(a='Ada')", folded)
        self.assertIn("greet('Grace', b='?')", folded)
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_keyword_call_before_definition(self):
        source = (
            "def caller():\n"
            "    return helper(value=41)\n"
            "def helper(value):\n"
            "    return value + 1\n"
            "print(caller())\n"
        )
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_class_kwargs_rewritten(self):
        source = (
            "class Point:\n"
            "    def __init__(self, x, y):\n"
            "        self.x = x\n"
            "        self.y = y\n"
            "    def total(self):\n"
            "        return self.x + self.y\n"
            "print(Point(x=3, y=4).total())\n"
        )
        folded = self._obf(source)
        self.assertIn("Point(b=3, c=4)", folded)
        self.assertIn("__init__", folded)
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_dunder_methods_kept(self):
        source = (
            "class C:\n"
            "    def __init__(self):\n"
            "        self.v = 1\n"
            "    def __str__(self):\n"
            "        return 'C:' + str(self.v)\n"
            "print(str(C()))\n"
        )
        folded = self._obf(source)
        self.assertIn("def __init__(a):", folded)
        self.assertIn("def __str__(b):", folded)
        check_preserved(source, Obfuscator)

    def test_preserves_runtime_values(self):
        for name, source in DEMO_PROGRAMS.items():
            with self.subTest(program=name):
                check_preserved(source, Obfuscator, label=name)

    def test_shorter_output(self):
        source = DEMO_PROGRAMS["statistics"]
        obfuscated, _ = obfuscate_source(source)
        self.assertLess(len(obfuscated), len(source))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    demo()
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("Unit tests")
    print("=" * 70)
    unittest.main(argv=[sys.argv[0], "-v"], exit=True)

--- SOURCE: python_samples/transform_annotations.py ---
"""Type-annotation adder: infer simple annotations from literals.

This module implements a source-to-source AST transformer that adds
type annotations where they can be inferred safely from the code
itself:

* ``x = 5`` becomes ``x: int = 5``; ``name = "Ada"`` becomes
  ``name: str = "Ada"``; ``items = [1, 2]`` becomes
  ``items: list[int] = [1, 2]``; dicts, sets and tuples are inferred
  the same way, with uniform element types.
* Function parameters with literal defaults gain annotations:
  ``def greet(name="Ada")`` becomes ``def greet(name: str = "Ada")``.
* A function whose every ``return`` yields the same inferred type
  gains a return annotation: ``def double(n): return n * 2`` stays
  unannotated (``n * 2`` is not a literal), but a function returning
  only string literals becomes ``-> str``.

How inference works:

* ``_infer_type`` maps a literal (or a simple combination of
  literals, such as ``1 + 2`` or ``"a" + "b"``) to a type string.
  ``bool`` is checked before ``int`` because ``bool`` is a subclass
  of ``int``; a mixed or empty collection falls back to the bare
  generic (``list``, ``dict``), and anything that cannot be inferred
  is left alone.
* A name is annotated only when *every* assignment to it in the same
  scope infers the *same* type.  One conflicting assignment vetoes
  the annotation for that name.
* Names used as loop targets, ``with ... as`` targets, exception
  handler names, or augmented-assignment targets are never annotated:
  their type can change across the scope.
* Names that already have an annotation are left alone.

Safety rules:

* Only builtin types are ever written (``int``, ``str``, ``list``,
  ``dict``, ``set``, ``tuple``, ``float``, ``bool``, ``bytes``,
  ``None``), so an annotation can never fail to resolve at run time.
  Module- and class-level annotations *are* evaluated by Python, so
  this matters; local variable annotations are not evaluated, but
  the same rule keeps the output uniform.
* Annotations never change behaviour: ``x: int = 5`` assigns exactly
  what ``x = 5`` assigns.  The demo verifies this by executing each
  program before and after annotation and comparing stdout, and by
  checking that the annotations actually appear in the output.

Run directly::

    python3 transform_annotations.py
"""

import ast
import contextlib
import io
import sys
import unittest

__all__ = ["AnnotationAdder", "add_annotations", "run_source",
           "check_preserved"]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small shared helpers (duplicated in each transform_* file so every file
# runs standalone).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_source(source, extra_globals=None):
    """Execute *source* and return ``(stdout, namespace)``."""
    namespace = {} if extra_globals is None else dict(extra_globals)
    buffer = io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buffer):
        exec(compile(source, "<demo>", "exec"), namespace)
    return buffer.getvalue(), namespace


def check_preserved(source, transformer, label=""):
    """Transform *source* and assert identical observable behaviour.

    Returns ``(new_source, original_output, new_output)``.
    """
    original_out, _ = run_source(source)
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    new_tree = transformer().visit(tree)
    ast.fix_missing_locations(new_tree)
    new_source = ast.unparse(new_tree)
    compile(new_source, "<transformed>", "exec")
    new_out, _ = run_source(new_source)
    assert original_out == new_out, (
        "behaviour changed%s:\noriginal: %r\ntransformed: %r\n%s"
        % (" for " + label if label else "", original_out, new_out, new_source)
    )
    return new_source, original_out, new_out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Type inference.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _infer_type(node):
    """Infer a type string for *node*, or None when it cannot be known.

    Only literals and simple combinations of literals are inferred.
    Anything involving a name, a call to an unknown function, or a
    subscript returns None, so the transformer never guesses.
    """
    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
        value = node.value
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return "bool"
        if isinstance(value, int):
            return "int"
        if isinstance(value, float):
            return "float"
        if isinstance(value, complex):
            return "complex"
        if isinstance(value, str):
            return "str"
        if isinstance(value, bytes):
            return "bytes"
        if value is None:
            return "None"
        if value is Ellipsis:
            return None
        return None
    if isinstance(node, ast.List):
        if not node.elts:
            return "list"
        types = {_infer_type(elt) for elt in node.elts}
        if len(types) == 1 and None not in types:
            return "list[%s]" % types.pop()
        return "list"
    if isinstance(node, ast.Dict):
        if not node.keys:
            return "dict"
        key_types = {_infer_type(key) for key in node.keys if key is not None}
        value_types = {_infer_type(value) for value in node.values}
        if (len(key_types) == 1 and len(value_types) == 1
                and None not in key_types and None not in value_types):
            return "dict[%s, %s]" % (key_types.pop(), value_types.pop())
        return "dict"
    if isinstance(node, ast.Set):
        if not node.elts:
            return "set"
        types = {_infer_type(elt) for elt in node.elts}
        if len(types) == 1 and None not in types:
            return "set[%s]" % types.pop()
        return "set"
    if isinstance(node, ast.Tuple):
        if not node.elts:
            return "tuple[()]"
        types = [_infer_type(elt) for elt in node.elts]
        if any(t is None for t in types):
            return "tuple"
        if len(set(types)) == 1:
            return "tuple[%s, ...]" % types[0]
        if len(types) <= 4:
            return "tuple[%s]" % ", ".join(types)
        return "tuple"
    if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
        left = _infer_type(node.left)
        right = _infer_type(node.right)
        if left is None or right is None:
            return None
        if isinstance(node.op, ast.Add):
            if left == right == "str":
                return "str"
            if left in ("int", "float") and right in ("int", "float"):
                return "float" if "float" in (left, right) else "int"
            return None
        if isinstance(node.op, (ast.Sub, ast.Mult, ast.FloorDiv, ast.Mod)):
            if left in ("int", "float") and right in ("int", "float"):
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Mult) and left == right == "int":
                    return "int"
                return "float" if "float" in (left, right) else "int"
            return None
        if isinstance(node.op, ast.Div):
            if left in ("int", "float") and right in ("int", "float"):
                return "float"
            return None
        return None
    if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(
            node.op, (ast.UAdd, ast.USub)):
        inner = _infer_type(node.operand)
        return inner if inner in ("int", "float", "complex") else None
    if isinstance(node, ast.IfExp):
        body = _infer_type(node.body)
        orelse = _infer_type(node.orelse)
        if body is not None and body == orelse:
            return body
        return None
    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
        if (isinstance(node.func, ast.Name)
                and node.func.id in ("int", "float", "str", "bool",
                                     "bytes", "list", "dict", "set",
                                     "tuple")
                and not node.keywords):
            return node.func.id
        if (isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id == "len"
                and len(node.args) == 1 and not node.keywords):
            return "int"
        return None
    return None


def _parse_annotation(type_string, template):
    """Parse *type_string* into an annotation node located like *template*."""
    node = ast.parse(type_string, mode="eval").body
    return ast.copy_location(node, template)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scope analysis: which names have one consistent inferred type?
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _collect_assignments(stmts):
    """Analyse one scope's assignments.

    Returns ``(types, vetoed)`` where ``types`` maps a name to its
    single consistent inferred type and ``vetoed`` is the set of names
    that must never be annotated (reassigned with a different type,
    already annotated, or bound by a loop / with / except / ``+=``).
    """
    types = {}
    vetoed = set()

    def veto(name):
        vetoed.add(name)
        types.pop(name, None)

    def consider(name, node):
        inferred = _infer_type(node)
        if inferred is None:
            veto(name)
            return
        if name in types and types[name] != inferred:
            veto(name)
            return
        types[name] = inferred

    def walk(node):
        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef,
                             ast.ClassDef, ast.Lambda)):
            return  # a scope of its own
        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
            if (len(node.targets) == 1
                    and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name)):
                consider(node.targets[0].id, node.value)
            else:
                for target in node.targets:
                    for name in _target_names(target):
                        veto(name)
            walk(node.value)  # a walrus inside the value still binds here
            return
        if isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign):
            for name in _target_names(node.target):
                veto(name)  # already annotated: leave it alone
            if node.value is not None:
                walk(node.value)
            return
        if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
            for name in _target_names(node.target):
                veto(name)
            walk(node.value)
            return
        if isinstance(node, ast.NamedExpr):
            veto(node.target.id)
            walk(node.value)
            return
        if isinstance(node, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):
            for name in _target_names(node.target):
                veto(name)
        if isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and node.name:
            veto(node.name)
        if isinstance(node, ast.With):
            for item in node.items:
                if item.optional_vars is not None:
                    for name in _target_names(item.optional_vars):
                        veto(name)
        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
            walk(child)

    for stmt in stmts:
        walk(stmt)
    return types, vetoed


def _target_names(target):
    """Names bound by an assignment target (handles tuple unpacking)."""
    if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
        return [target.id]
    if isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List)):
        names = []
        for elt in target.elts:
            names.extend(_target_names(elt))
        return names
    if isinstance(target, ast.Starred):
        return _target_names(target.value)
    return []


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The transformer.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class AnnotationAdder(ast.NodeTransformer):
    """Add inferred type annotations to assignments and signatures."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.annotated = 0       # assignments converted to AnnAssign
        self.params_annotated = 0
        self.returns_annotated = 0
        # Stack of per-scope type maps; the innermost scope governs
        # each assignment.  Analysis is per *scope*, never per block:
        # a name assigned in two branches of an if must agree across
        # the whole function, not just within one branch.
        self._type_stack = []

    # -- one scope ---------------------------------------------------------------

    def _enter_scope(self, stmts):
        types, _vetoed = _collect_assignments(stmts)
        self._type_stack.append(types)

    def visit_Assign(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        if (self._type_stack and len(node.targets) == 1
                and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name)
                and node.targets[0].id in self._type_stack[-1]):
            name = node.targets[0].id
            ann = ast.AnnAssign(
                target=node.targets[0],
                annotation=_parse_annotation(self._type_stack[-1][name], node),
                value=node.value,
                simple=1,
            )
            ast.copy_location(ann, node)
            self.annotated += 1
            return ann
        return node

    def _annotate_function(self, node):
        # Parameters with literal defaults.
        args = node.args
        defaults = list(args.defaults)
        params = list(args.posonlyargs) + list(args.args)
        for param, default in zip(params[len(params) - len(defaults):],
                                  defaults):
            if param.annotation is None:
                inferred = _infer_type(default)
                if inferred is not None:
                    param.annotation = _parse_annotation(inferred, default)
                    self.params_annotated += 1
        for param, default in zip(args.kwonlyargs, args.kw_defaults):
            if (default is not None and param.annotation is None):
                inferred = _infer_type(default)
                if inferred is not None:
                    param.annotation = _parse_annotation(inferred, default)
                    self.params_annotated += 1
        # Return type: all returns must infer the same type, and there
        # must be no bare `return` mixed in.  Only this function's own
        # returns count -- nested functions are separate scopes.
        if node.returns is None:
            return_types = set()
            saw_bare_return = False
            stack = [node]
            while stack:
                current = stack.pop()
                if isinstance(current, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef,
                                        ast.ClassDef, ast.Lambda)):
                    if current is not node:
                        continue
                if isinstance(current, ast.Return):
                    if current.value is None:
                        saw_bare_return = True
                    else:
                        inferred = _infer_type(current.value)
                        if inferred is None:
                            saw_bare_return = True
                        else:
                            return_types.add(inferred)
                stack.extend(ast.iter_child_nodes(current))
            if not saw_bare_return and len(return_types) == 1:
                node.returns = _parse_annotation(return_types.pop(), node)
                self.returns_annotated += 1

    # -- NodeTransformer hooks -----------------------------------------------

    def visit_Module(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        return node

    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        self._annotate_function(node)
        return node

    def visit_AsyncFunctionDef(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        self._annotate_function(node)
        return node

    def visit_ClassDef(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        return node


def add_annotations(source):
    """Add inferred annotations to *source*; return ``(new_source, stats)``."""
    adder = AnnotationAdder()
    tree = adder.visit(ast.parse(source))
    ast.fix_missing_locations(tree)
    stats = {"annotated": adder.annotated,
             "params_annotated": adder.params_annotated,
             "returns_annotated": adder.returns_annotated}
    return ast.unparse(tree), stats


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Demonstration programs.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DEMO_PROGRAMS = {
    "config_and_math": '''
MAX_RETRIES = 3
TIMEOUT = 2.5
APP_NAME = "transformer-demo"
DEBUG = False
DEFAULT_TAGS = ["python", "ast"]
SCORES = {"alice": 95, "bob": 87}
EMPTY = []
RATIO = 1 + 2

def describe():
    label = "ready"
    count = MAX_RETRIES * 2
    return label, count

print(MAX_RETRIES, TIMEOUT, APP_NAME, DEBUG)
print(DEFAULT_TAGS, SCORES, EMPTY, RATIO)
print(describe())
''',
    "functions_with_defaults": '''
def greet(name="world", punctuation="!"):
    return "Hello, " + name + punctuation

def add(a=0, b=0):
    return a + b

def is_ready(flag=True):
    if flag:
        return "yes"
    return "no"

def label_for(score=0):
    if score >= 90:
        return "A"
    return "B"

print(greet())
print(greet("Ada", "?"))
print(add(3, 4))
print(is_ready())
print(label_for(95))
print(label_for(10))
''',
    "collections_and_branches": '''
def build_report(title="report"):
    lines = ["header", title]
    total = 0
    counts = {}
    for word in ["one", "two", "three"]:
        counts[word] = len(word)
        total += len(word)
    if total > 5:
        status = "long"
    else:
        status = "short"
    return lines, counts, total, status

def first_or_none(items=None):
    if items is None:
        return "empty"
    return items

print(build_report())
print(build_report("summary"))
print(first_or_none())
print(first_or_none(["x"]))
''',
    "class_attributes": '''
class Settings:
    retries = 3
    timeout = 1.5
    name = "default"

    def describe(self):
        label = "settings"
        return label + ": " + self.name

config = Settings()
print(config.retries, config.timeout, config.name)
print(config.describe())
''',
}


def demo():
    """Run every demo program through the annotator and report."""
    print("=" * 70)
    print("Type-annotation adder demo")
    print("=" * 70)
    for name, source in DEMO_PROGRAMS.items():
        annotated, stats = add_annotations(source)
        new_source, original_out, _ = check_preserved(
            source, AnnotationAdder, label=name)
        print("\n--- %s: %d assignment(s), %d param(s), %d return(s) "
              "annotated ---"
              % (name, stats["annotated"], stats["params_annotated"],
                 stats["returns_annotated"]))
        print(annotated.rstrip())
        assert " : " in annotated or ": " in annotated, (
            "no annotations were added for " + name)
    print("\nAll behaviour-preservation checks passed.")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Unit tests.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestAnnotationAdder(unittest.TestCase):
    def _add(self, source):
        return add_annotations(source)[0]

    def test_literals(self):
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = 5\n"), "x: int = 5")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = 2.5\n"), "x: float = 2.5")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = 'hi'\n"), "x: str = 'hi'")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = True\n"), "x: bool = True")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = None\n"), "x: None = None")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = b'bytes'\n"), "x: bytes = b'bytes'")

    def test_collections(self):
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = [1, 2, 3]\n"), "x: list[int] = [1, 2, 3]")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = []\n"), "x: list = []")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = {'a': 1}\n"), "x: dict[str, int] = {'a': 1}")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = {}\n"), "x: dict = {}")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = {1, 2}\n"), "x: set[int] = {1, 2}")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = (1, 'a')\n"),
                         "x: tuple[int, str] = (1, 'a')")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = (1, 2)\n"),
                         "x: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 2)")

    def test_mixed_collection_falls_back(self):
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = [1, 'a']\n"), "x: list = [1, 'a']")

    def test_no_annotation_for_names(self):
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = other\n"), "x = other")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = compute()\n"), "x = compute()")

    def test_conflicting_assignments_veto(self):
        folded = self._add("x = 5\nx = 'hi'\n")
        self.assertEqual(folded, "x = 5\nx = 'hi'")

    def test_consistent_reassignment_annotated(self):
        folded = self._add("x = 5\nx = 10\n")
        self.assertEqual(folded, "x: int = 5\nx: int = 10")

    def test_loop_target_not_annotated(self):
        folded = self._add("for i in range(3):\n    print(i)\n")
        self.assertNotIn("i: int", folded)

    def test_augassign_not_annotated(self):
        folded = self._add("x = 0\nx += 1\n")
        self.assertEqual(folded, "x = 0\nx += 1")

    def test_existing_annotation_kept(self):
        folded = self._add("x: str = 'hi'\n")
        self.assertEqual(folded, "x: str = 'hi'")

    def test_params_with_defaults(self):
        folded = self._add("def f(name='Ada', n=3):\n    return name\n")
        self.assertEqual(folded,
                         "def f(name: str='Ada', n: int=3):\n    return name")

    def test_return_type_uniform(self):
        folded = self._add(
            "def f(flag):\n    if flag:\n        return 'yes'\n    return 'no'\n")
        self.assertIn("-> str", folded)

    def test_return_type_mixed_not_annotated(self):
        folded = self._add(
            "def f(flag):\n    if flag:\n        return 'yes'\n    return 0\n")
        self.assertNotIn("->", folded)

    def test_bare_return_blocks_annotation(self):
        folded = self._add("def f(flag):\n    if flag:\n        return\n    return 'x'\n")
        self.assertNotIn("->", folded)

    def test_binop_inference(self):
        # The value is kept as written; only the annotation is added.
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = 1 + 2\n"), "x: int = 1 + 2")
        self.assertEqual(self._add("x = 1.5 + 2\n"), "x: float = 1.5 + 2")

    def test_branch_conflict_vetoes_everywhere(self):
        # x is str in one branch and int in the other: no annotation
        # anywhere, not even inside the single-type branch.
        folded = self._add(
            "def f(flag):\n"
            "    if flag:\n"
            "        x = 'hi'\n"
            "    else:\n"
            "        x = 5\n"
            "    return x\n")
        self.assertNotIn("x: ", folded)

    def test_nested_function_returns_ignored(self):
        folded = self._add(
            "def f():\n"
            "    def g():\n"
            "        return 'inner'\n"
            "    return g()\n")
        self.assertNotIn("def f() ->", folded)
        self.assertIn("def g() -> str:", folded)

    def test_module_annotations_are_real(self):
        _, namespace = run_source(self._add("x = 5\nname = 'Ada'\n"))
        self.assertEqual(namespace["__annotations__"],
                         {"x": int, "name": str})

    def test_preserves_runtime_values(self):
        for name, source in DEMO_PROGRAMS.items():
            with self.subTest(program=name):
                check_preserved(source, AnnotationAdder, label=name)

    def test_stats_reported(self):
        _, stats = add_annotations("x = 5\ndef f(n=0):\n    return 'a'\n")
        self.assertEqual(stats["annotated"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(stats["params_annotated"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(stats["returns_annotated"], 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    demo()
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("Unit tests")
    print("=" * 70)
    unittest.main(argv=[sys.argv[0], "-v"], exit=True)

--- SOURCE: python_samples/transform_pipeline.py ---
"""Transformer pipeline: chain several AST passes and verify the result.

This module chains four of the transformers from this series into one
pipeline -- constant folding, dead-code elimination, loop optimisation
and type-annotation adding -- and proves that the composed result still
behaves identically to the original program.  The four stages are
implemented here in compact form (each is a faithful, working version
of the corresponding ``transform_*.py`` file, which carries the full
implementation and its own tests); the point of this file is the
composition: stage ordering, per-stage reporting, and end-to-end
verification of the whole chain.

Why the stages run in this order:

1. Constant folding first, because it turns ``if False:`` and
   ``while False:`` into dead code and turns ``2 * 5`` into ``10``,
   which the later stages can then use.
2. Dead-code elimination second, because folding exposes new dead
   code (a folded ``if True:`` can leave a ``return`` as the last
   statement of a block, which kills everything after the ``if``).
3. Loop optimisation third, because it works on the code that
   survived: hoisting an invariant out of a loop that dead-code
   elimination was about to delete would be wasted work, and the
   loop pass itself benefits from folded constants in bounds and
   step values.
4. Type-annotation adding last, because annotations describe the
   final code: annotating first and transforming later could leave
   an annotation on a statement another stage deletes, or -- worse --
   an annotation whose inferred type no longer matches the folded
   value.

The ``Pipeline`` class runs each stage in turn, records the source
size and the number of changes each stage made, and verifies after
*every* stage -- not just at the end -- that the program still
produces identical output.  If any stage ever changed the behaviour,
the pipeline stops and reports which stage broke it, instead of
silently passing a broken program on to the next stage.

Run directly::

    python3 transform_pipeline.py
"""

import ast
import contextlib
import io
import operator
import sys
import unittest

__all__ = ["Pipeline", "run_pipeline", "run_source", "check_preserved"]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small shared helpers (duplicated in each transform_* file so every file
# runs standalone).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_source(source, extra_globals=None):
    """Execute *source* and return ``(stdout, namespace)``."""
    namespace = {} if extra_globals is None else dict(extra_globals)
    buffer = io.StringIO()
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(buffer):
        exec(compile(source, "<demo>", "exec"), namespace)
    return buffer.getvalue(), namespace


def check_preserved(source, transformer, label=""):
    """Transform *source* and assert identical observable behaviour.

    Returns ``(new_source, original_output, new_output)``.
    """
    original_out, _ = run_source(source)
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    new_tree = transformer().visit(tree)
    ast.fix_missing_locations(new_tree)
    new_source = ast.unparse(new_tree)
    compile(new_source, "<transformed>", "exec")
    new_out, _ = run_source(new_source)
    assert original_out == new_out, (
        "behaviour changed%s:\noriginal: %r\ntransformed: %r\n%s"
        % (" for " + label if label else "", original_out, new_out, new_source)
    )
    return new_source, original_out, new_out


def _literal(node):
    try:
        return ast.literal_eval(node)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, SyntaxError, MemoryError, RecursionError):
        return _MISSING


_MISSING = object()


def _loaded_names(node):
    return {child.id for child in ast.walk(node)
            if isinstance(child, ast.Name)
            and isinstance(child.ctx, ast.Load)}


def _assigned_in(node):
    assigned = set()
    stack = [node]
    while stack:
        current = stack.pop()
        if isinstance(current, ast.comprehension):
            stack.extend(current.ifs)
            stack.append(current.iter)
            continue
        if isinstance(current, ast.Name) and isinstance(current.ctx, ast.Store):
            assigned.add(current.id)
        if isinstance(current, ast.ExceptHandler) and current.name:
            assigned.add(current.name)
        stack.extend(ast.iter_child_nodes(current))
    return assigned


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 1 -- constant folding (compact version of transform_folding.py).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_BINOPS = {
    ast.Add: operator.add, ast.Sub: operator.sub, ast.Mult: operator.mul,
    ast.Div: operator.truediv, ast.FloorDiv: operator.floordiv,
    ast.Mod: operator.mod, ast.Pow: operator.pow,
    ast.LShift: operator.lshift, ast.RShift: operator.rshift,
    ast.BitOr: operator.or_, ast.BitXor: operator.xor,
    ast.BitAnd: operator.and_,
}

_UNOPS = {
    ast.UAdd: operator.pos, ast.USub: operator.neg,
    ast.Invert: operator.invert, ast.Not: operator.not_,
}

_COMPARATORS = {
    ast.Eq: operator.eq, ast.NotEq: operator.ne, ast.Lt: operator.lt,
    ast.LtE: operator.le, ast.Gt: operator.gt, ast.GtE: operator.ge,
    ast.In: lambda a, b: a in b, ast.NotIn: lambda a, b: a not in b,
}


class _Folder(ast.NodeTransformer):
    """Fold constant expressions; see transform_folding.py for the full version."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.changes = 0

    def _const(self, value, template):
        self.changes += 1
        return ast.copy_location(ast.Constant(value=value), template)

    def visit_BinOp(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        left, right = _literal(node.left), _literal(node.right)
        func = _BINOPS.get(type(node.op))
        if left is _MISSING or right is _MISSING or func is None:
            return node
        if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow) and isinstance(right, (int, float)) \
                and abs(right) > 64:
            return node
        try:
            value = func(left, right)
        except Exception:
            return node
        if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) \
                and value.bit_length() > 4096:
            return node
        if isinstance(value, (str, bytes)) and len(value) > 100_000:
            return node
        return self._const(value, node)

    def visit_UnaryOp(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        operand = _literal(node.operand)
        func = _UNOPS.get(type(node.op))
        if operand is _MISSING or func is None:
            return node
        try:
            return self._const(func(operand), node)
        except Exception:
            return node

    def visit_BoolOp(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        values = [_literal(v) for v in node.values]
        if any(v is _MISSING for v in values):
            return node
        if isinstance(node.op, ast.And):
            result = values[0]
            for value in values[1:]:
                if not result:
                    break
                result = value
        else:
            result = values[0]
            for value in values[1:]:
                if result:
                    break
                result = value
        return self._const(result, node)

    def visit_Compare(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        left = _literal(node.left)
        comparators = [_literal(c) for c in node.comparators]
        if left is _MISSING or any(c is _MISSING for c in comparators):
            return node
        current = left
        try:
            for op, nxt in zip(node.ops, comparators):
                func = _COMPARATORS.get(type(op))
                if func is None or not func(current, nxt):
                    return self._const(False, node)
                current = nxt
        except Exception:
            return node
        return self._const(True, node)

    def visit_IfExp(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        test = _literal(node.test)
        if test is _MISSING:
            return node
        self.changes += 1
        return node.body if test else node.orelse

    def visit_If(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        test = _literal(node.test)
        if test is _MISSING:
            return node
        self.changes += 1
        return node.body if test else node.orelse

    def visit_While(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        test = _literal(node.test)
        if test is _MISSING or test:
            return node
        self.changes += 1
        return []


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 2 -- dead-code elimination (compact version of transform_deadcode.py).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _DeadCode(ast.NodeTransformer):
    """Delete unreachable statements; see transform_deadcode.py."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.changes = 0

    def _terminates(self, stmt):
        if isinstance(stmt, (ast.Return, ast.Raise, ast.Break, ast.Continue)):
            return True
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.Assert):
            value = _literal(stmt.test)
            return value is not _MISSING and not value
        if isinstance(stmt, ast.If):
            return (bool(stmt.orelse)
                    and self._block_terminates(stmt.body)
                    and self._block_terminates(stmt.orelse))
        if isinstance(stmt, (ast.Try, getattr(ast, "TryStar", ast.Try))):
            return (self._block_terminates(stmt.body)
                    and all(self._block_terminates(h.body)
                            for h in stmt.handlers))
        return False

    def _block_terminates(self, stmts):
        return bool(stmts) and self._terminates(stmts[-1])

    def _process(self, stmts):
        out = []
        for stmt in stmts:
            out.append(stmt)
            if self._terminates(stmt):
                dropped = stmts[len(out):]
                self.changes += len(dropped)
                break
        return out

    def _prune(self, node):
        changed = False
        for field, value in ast.iter_fields(node):
            if isinstance(value, list) and value and all(
                    isinstance(item, ast.stmt) for item in value):
                new_value = self._process(value)
                if len(new_value) != len(value):
                    setattr(node, field, new_value)
                    changed = True
                for item in new_value:
                    changed = self._prune(item) or changed
            elif isinstance(value, ast.AST):
                changed = self._prune(value) or changed
        return changed

    def visit_Module(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        for _ in range(10):
            if not self._prune(node):
                break
        return node


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 3 -- loop optimisation (compact version of transform_loops.py).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _is_pure(node, invariant):
    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
        return True
    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
        return isinstance(node.ctx, ast.Load) and node.id in invariant
    if isinstance(node, (ast.BinOp, ast.UnaryOp, ast.BoolOp, ast.IfExp)):
        return all(_is_pure(c, invariant) for c in ast.iter_child_nodes(node))
    if isinstance(node, ast.Subscript):
        return _is_pure(node.value, invariant) and _is_pure(node.slice, invariant)
    if isinstance(node, (ast.List, ast.Tuple, ast.Set)):
        return all(_is_pure(e, invariant) for e in node.elts)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
        return (isinstance(node.func, ast.Name)
                and node.func.id in ("len", "abs")
                and not node.keywords
                and all(_is_pure(a, invariant) for a in node.args))
    return False


class _LoopOpt(ast.NodeTransformer):
    """Hoist invariants and reduce strength; see transform_loops.py."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.changes = 0

    def visit_BinOp(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        if (isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow)
                and isinstance(node.right, ast.Constant)
                and node.right.value == 2
                and not isinstance(node.left, ast.Constant)):
            import copy
            self.changes += 1
            return ast.copy_location(
                ast.BinOp(left=node.left, op=ast.Mult(),
                          right=copy.deepcopy(node.left)), node)
        if isinstance(node.op, ast.Mult):
            for const_side, other in ((node.right, node.left),
                                      (node.left, node.right)):
                if (isinstance(const_side, ast.Constant)
                        and const_side.value == 2
                        and not isinstance(other, ast.Constant)):
                    import copy
                    self.changes += 1
                    return ast.copy_location(
                        ast.BinOp(left=other, op=ast.Add(),
                                  right=copy.deepcopy(other)), node)
        return node

    def _hoist(self, loop, read_after):
        assigned = _assigned_in(loop)
        header_reads = (_loaded_names(loop.iter) if isinstance(loop, ast.For)
                        else _loaded_names(loop.test))
        counts = {}
        for child in ast.walk(loop):
            if isinstance(child, ast.Name) and isinstance(child.ctx, ast.Store):
                counts[child.id] = counts.get(child.id, 0) + 1
        hoisted, kept, read_before = [], [], set()
        for stmt in loop.body:
            if (isinstance(stmt, ast.Assign) and len(stmt.targets) == 1
                    and isinstance(stmt.targets[0], ast.Name)):
                target = stmt.targets[0].id
                loaded = _loaded_names(stmt.value)
                invariant = {n for n in loaded if n not in assigned}
                if (target not in read_after and target not in read_before
                        and target not in header_reads
                        and counts.get(target, 0) == 1
                        and loaded <= invariant
                        and _is_pure(stmt.value, invariant)
                        and target not in loaded):
                    hoisted.append(stmt)
                    self.changes += 1
                    continue
            kept.append(stmt)
            read_before |= _loaded_names(stmt)
        loop.body = kept
        return hoisted

    def _optimise_block(self, stmts, read_after=frozenset()):
        out = []
        for index, stmt in enumerate(stmts):
            after = set(read_after)
            for later in stmts[index + 1:]:
                after |= _loaded_names(later)
            if isinstance(stmt, (ast.For, ast.While)):
                loop_after = set(after)
                for else_stmt in getattr(stmt, "orelse", []):
                    loop_after |= _loaded_names(else_stmt)
                out.extend(self._hoist(stmt, loop_after))
                stmt.body = self._optimise_block(stmt.body, loop_after)
                stmt.orelse = self._optimise_block(stmt.orelse, after)
                out.append(stmt)
            elif isinstance(stmt, ast.If):
                stmt.body = self._optimise_block(stmt.body, after)
                stmt.orelse = self._optimise_block(stmt.orelse, after)
                out.append(stmt)
            elif isinstance(stmt, (ast.Try, getattr(ast, "TryStar", ast.Try))):
                stmt.body = self._optimise_block(stmt.body, after)
                stmt.orelse = self._optimise_block(stmt.orelse, after)
                for handler in stmt.handlers:
                    handler.body = self._optimise_block(handler.body, after)
                stmt.finalbody = self._optimise_block(stmt.finalbody, after)
                out.append(stmt)
            else:
                out.append(stmt)
        return out

    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        node.body = self._optimise_block(node.body)
        return node

    def visit_AsyncFunctionDef(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        node.body = self._optimise_block(node.body)
        return node

    def visit_Module(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        node.body = self._optimise_block(node.body)
        return node


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stage 4 -- annotation adding (compact version of transform_annotations.py).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _infer(node):
    if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
        value = node.value
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return "bool"
        if isinstance(value, int):
            return "int"
        if isinstance(value, float):
            return "float"
        if isinstance(value, str):
            return "str"
        if isinstance(value, bytes):
            return "bytes"
        if value is None:
            return "None"
        return None
    if isinstance(node, ast.List):
        if not node.elts:
            return "list"
        types = {_infer(e) for e in node.elts}
        if len(types) == 1 and None not in types:
            return "list[%s]" % types.pop()
        return "list"
    if isinstance(node, ast.Dict):
        if not node.keys:
            return "dict"
        keys = {_infer(k) for k in node.keys if k is not None}
        values = {_infer(v) for v in node.values}
        if (len(keys) == 1 and len(values) == 1
                and None not in keys and None not in values):
            return "dict[%s, %s]" % (keys.pop(), values.pop())
        return "dict"
    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
        if (isinstance(node.func, ast.Name)
                and node.func.id in ("int", "float", "str", "bool", "list",
                                     "dict", "set", "tuple")
                and not node.keywords):
            return node.func.id
        return None
    return None


def _target_names(target):
    if isinstance(target, ast.Name):
        return [target.id]
    if isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List)):
        names = []
        for elt in target.elts:
            names.extend(_target_names(elt))
        return names
    return []


class _Annotator(ast.NodeTransformer):
    """Add inferred annotations; see transform_annotations.py."""

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.changes = 0
        self._type_stack = []

    def _enter_scope(self, stmts):
        types, vetoed = {}, set()
        for stmt in stmts:
            self._scan(stmt, types, vetoed)
        self._type_stack.append(types)

    def _scan(self, node, types, vetoed):
        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef,
                             ast.ClassDef, ast.Lambda)):
            return
        if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
            if (len(node.targets) == 1
                    and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name)):
                name = node.targets[0].id
                inferred = _infer(node.value)
                if inferred is None or name in vetoed:
                    vetoed.add(name)
                    types.pop(name, None)
                elif name in types and types[name] != inferred:
                    vetoed.add(name)
                    types.pop(name, None)
                else:
                    types[name] = inferred
            else:
                for target in node.targets:
                    for name in _target_names(target):
                        vetoed.add(name)
                        types.pop(name, None)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign):
            for name in _target_names(node.target):
                vetoed.add(name)
                types.pop(name, None)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
            for name in _target_names(node.target):
                vetoed.add(name)
                types.pop(name, None)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.NamedExpr):
            vetoed.add(node.target.id)
            types.pop(node.target.id, None)
        elif isinstance(node, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):
            for name in _target_names(node.target):
                vetoed.add(name)
                types.pop(name, None)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and node.name:
            vetoed.add(node.name)
            types.pop(node.name, None)
        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
            self._scan(child, types, vetoed)

    def visit_Assign(self, node):
        node = self.generic_visit(node)
        if (self._type_stack and len(node.targets) == 1
                and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name)
                and node.targets[0].id in self._type_stack[-1]):
            name = node.targets[0].id
            ann = ast.AnnAssign(
                target=node.targets[0],
                annotation=ast.copy_location(
                    ast.parse(self._type_stack[-1][name], mode="eval").body,
                    node),
                value=node.value,
                simple=1,
            )
            ast.copy_location(ann, node)
            self.changes += 1
            return ann
        return node

    def _annotate_function(self, node):
        args = node.args
        defaults = list(args.defaults)
        params = list(args.posonlyargs) + list(args.args)
        for param, default in zip(params[len(params) - len(defaults):],
                                  defaults):
            if param.annotation is None:
                inferred = _infer(default)
                if inferred is not None:
                    param.annotation = ast.copy_location(
                        ast.parse(inferred, mode="eval").body, default)
                    self.changes += 1
        for param, default in zip(args.kwonlyargs, args.kw_defaults):
            if default is not None and param.annotation is None:
                inferred = _infer(default)
                if inferred is not None:
                    param.annotation = ast.copy_location(
                        ast.parse(inferred, mode="eval").body, default)
                    self.changes += 1

    def visit_Module(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        return node

    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        self._annotate_function(node)
        return node

    def visit_AsyncFunctionDef(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        self._annotate_function(node)
        return node

    def visit_ClassDef(self, node):
        self._enter_scope(node.body)
        self.generic_visit(node)
        self._type_stack.pop()
        return node


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The pipeline.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

STAGES = [
    ("constant folding", _Folder),
    ("dead-code elimination", _DeadCode),
    ("loop optimisation", _LoopOpt),
    ("annotation adding", _Annotator),
]


class Pipeline:
    """Run the four stages in order, verifying behaviour after each one."""

    def __init__(self, stages=None):
        self.stages = stages if stages is not None else STAGES
        self.report = []   # one dict per stage after a run

    def run(self, source):
        """Run the pipeline on *source*.

        Returns ``(final_source, report)`` where each report entry has
        the stage name, the number of changes it made, and the source
        size before and after.  Raises ``AssertionError`` naming the
        stage if any stage changes the program's observable behaviour.
        """
        current = source
        self.report = []
        for name, transformer_cls in self.stages:
            before_out, _ = run_source(current)
            transformer = transformer_cls()
            tree = transformer.visit(ast.parse(current))
            ast.fix_missing_locations(tree)
            new_source = ast.unparse(tree)
            compile(new_source, "<pipeline>", "exec")
            after_out, _ = run_source(new_source)
            if before_out != after_out:
                raise AssertionError(
                    "pipeline stage %r changed behaviour:\n"
                    "before: %r\nafter: %r\n%s"
                    % (name, before_out, after_out, new_source))
            self.report.append({
                "stage": name,
                "changes": transformer.changes,
                "chars_before": len(current),
                "chars_after": len(new_source),
            })
            current = new_source
        return current, self.report


def run_pipeline(source):
    """Run the default pipeline; return ``(final_source, report)``."""
    pipeline = Pipeline()
    return pipeline.run(source)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Demonstration programs.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

DEMO_PROGRAMS = {
    "everything_at_once": '''
DEBUG = False
LIMIT = 10 * 5
LABEL = "result" + "!"

def analyse(values):
    total = 0
    count = len(values)
    for value in values:
        weight = len(values)
        total += value ** 2 + weight
    if DEBUG:
        print("debug: total is", total)
    return total

def unused_helper():
    return 1
    print("never runs")

print(LABEL)
print(analyse([1, 2, 3, 4]))
print(analyse([]))
print("limit:", LIMIT)
''',
    "branchy_and_loopy": '''
THRESHOLD = 3 * 7
MODE = "fast"

def grade(scores):
    total = 0
    for score in scores:
        doubled = score * 2
        total += doubled
    if total >= THRESHOLD:
        verdict = "pass"
    else:
        verdict = "fail"
    return verdict, total

def show(items):
    for i in range(len(items)):
        print(i, items[i])
    return "done"

print(grade([5, 6, 7]))
print(grade([1, 1]))
print(show(["a", "b", "c"]))
print("mode:", MODE)
''',
    "nested_functions": '''
FACTOR = 2 * 3

def outer(numbers):
    def square(n):
        return n * n
    total = 0
    for number in numbers:
        scaled = number * FACTOR
        total += square(number) + scaled
    return total

def greet(name="world"):
    message = "Hello, " + name
    return message

print(outer([1, 2, 3]))
print(greet())
print(greet("Ada"))
print("factor:", FACTOR)
''',
}


def demo():
    """Run every demo program through the pipeline and report."""
    print("=" * 70)
    print("Transformer pipeline demo")
    print("=" * 70)
    for name, source in DEMO_PROGRAMS.items():
        final, report = run_pipeline(source)
        original_out, _ = run_source(source)
        final_out, _ = run_source(final)
        assert original_out == final_out, "pipeline broke " + name
        print("\n--- %s ---" % name)
        print("%-24s %8s %12s %12s" % ("stage", "changes", "chars before",
                                       "chars after"))
        for entry in report:
            print("%-24s %8d %12d %12d" % (
                entry["stage"], entry["changes"],
                entry["chars_before"], entry["chars_after"]))
        print("original chars: %d -> final chars: %d"
              % (len(source), len(final)))
        print(final.rstrip())
    print("\nAll pipelines preserved behaviour at every stage.")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Unit tests.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestPipeline(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_full_run_preserves_behaviour(self):
        for name, source in DEMO_PROGRAMS.items():
            with self.subTest(program=name):
                final, _ = run_pipeline(source)
                original_out, _ = run_source(source)
                final_out, _ = run_source(final)
                self.assertEqual(original_out, final_out)

    def test_stage_order_matters(self):
        # Folding must run before dead-code elimination: folding turns
        # `if False:` into nothing for the dead-code stage to remove.
        # Here the folded branch exposes a return that kills later code.
        source = (
            "def f():\n"
            "    if False:\n"
            "        return 1\n"
            "    else:\n"
            "        return 2\n"
            "    x = 3\n"
            "print(f())\n"
        )
        final, report = run_pipeline(source)
        self.assertNotIn("x = 3", final)
        self.assertIn("return 2", final)
        out, _ = run_source(final)
        self.assertEqual(out, "2\n")

    def test_annotations_describe_final_code(self):
        source = "LIMIT = 10 * 5\nprint(LIMIT)\n"
        final, _ = run_pipeline(source)
        self.assertIn("LIMIT: int = 50", final)
        out, _ = run_source(final)
        self.assertEqual(out, "50\n")

    def test_each_stage_verified_separately(self):
        # A pipeline that stops at a misbehaving stage reports it.
        class _BreakingStage(ast.NodeTransformer):
            changes = 0

            def visit_Constant(self, node):
                if node.value == 1:
                    return ast.copy_location(ast.Constant(value=99), node)
                return node

        pipeline = Pipeline(stages=[("breaking stage", _BreakingStage)])
        with self.assertRaises(AssertionError) as caught:
            pipeline.run("print(1)\n")
        self.assertIn("breaking stage", str(caught.exception))

    def test_empty_program(self):
        final, report = run_pipeline("x = 1\nprint(x)\n")
        out, _ = run_source(final)
        self.assertEqual(out, "1\n")
        self.assertEqual(len(report), 4)

    def test_report_counts(self):
        _, report = run_pipeline(DEMO_PROGRAMS["everything_at_once"])
        stages = [entry["stage"] for entry in report]
        self.assertEqual(stages, [name for name, _ in STAGES])
        total_changes = sum(entry["changes"] for entry in report)
        self.assertGreater(total_changes, 0)

    def test_loop_stage_hoists_in_pipeline(self):
        source = (
            "def f(items):\n"
            "    total = 0\n"
            "    for item in items:\n"
            "        n = len(items)\n"
            "        total += n\n"
            "    return total\n"
            "print(f([1, 2, 3]))\n"
        )
        final, report = run_pipeline(source)
        loop_entry = [e for e in report if e["stage"] == "loop optimisation"][0]
        self.assertGreaterEqual(loop_entry["changes"], 1)
        out, _ = run_source(final)
        self.assertEqual(out, "9\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    demo()
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("Unit tests")
    print("=" * 70)
    unittest.main(argv=[sys.argv[0], "-v"], exit=True)
